# Sparse breadth progress - 25 November 2026

> **Latest reconciled continuation through Batch1586, after seven trigger-oracle withdrawals:** 5,675 runnable scenarios (3,143 two-live +2,532 four-live), 2,697/3,324 represented groups. Historical snapshot PASS is not sufficient semantic credit. [Current checkpoint](BREADTH_FROM1457_20261124.md).






















> **20 November remaining-repair review pause:**4,188 qualified/registered (3,074 duel +1,114 four-live);1,339/5,196 identities and1,213/3,324 representative groups.81/93 original records resolved in tested scope,12open;125/147partial. [Current handoff](REPAIR_REMAINING17_20261120.md). No full suite or breadth expansion.


> **25 November, latest registration sparse1586-20261122:** 5,675 saved/registered scenarios (3,143 duels +2532 four-live), 2823 recorded primary IDs/5,196 and 2697 groups/3,324. This phase qualified 2312 scenarios across 2302 groups at their stated runtimes. 9 new gameplay defect groups fixed, plus 20 existing-family extensions. Qualified named repairs 085, 086, 165, 166, 167, 168, 169, 170, 171, 172, 173, 174, 175 remain tracked separately while global defect-group attribution is unallocated. No full-suite run for these creation batches; historical capped-suite evidence remains separate. [Progress and current lookup](SPARSE_PROGRESS_20261025.md) preserves exact branches, untested siblings and historical contexts. Current runtime `54409edb1ba1e0691c3beeb648e8ce984a09074b3339b7e303e53c61154e1494`; [latest checkpoint](card-regression-sparse1586-20261122-qualified-checkpoint.json) and [small additions index](coverage-additions-after218.json) map 2246 later scenarios /2235 groups. Full card/cycle indexes stay frozen through218.

**Current native holds, 3 November (073-079):**

- [073 Tremor](review-evidence/tremor073-duplicate-power-native-before.json): duplicate Monarch/reprint +2 paths produce power8 instead of6 after40 real inputs; no Tremor damage suffix executed.
- [074 Data Doll](review-evidence/datadoll074-trigger-native-before.json): actual boosted Item enters Arena inline after3 inputs without its printed responseable trigger; existing trigger-producer family.
- [075 Entwine Earth](review-evidence/entwine075-stale-fusion-native-before.json): after7 inputs, fusion still references removed Hand1 while Earth UID7 persists at Hand0; existing025 save-compaction family, distinct from026 recorder observation.
- [076 Pulping](review-evidence/pulping076-trigger-native-before.json): after7 inputs, draw/discard and DOMINATE occurred inline without the printed WHEN-attacks trigger response layer; no attack damage or go-again suffix executed.
- [077 Assault and Battery](review-evidence/assault077-stale-beat-chest-native-before.json): after2 inputs, optional Beat Chest still offers missing Hand2 instead of eligible UID7 at Hand1; existing025/075 save-compaction family. No choice/payment/discard/Agility/damage executed; later timing concerns remain source-only.

- [078 Ebon Fold](review-evidence/ebon078-premature-effect-choice-native-before.json): after one activation, its Hand-banish chooser is offered in Prepare while resource1 is unpaid and Head5 remains ready; existing060/039 phase-family caller. No payment, response, banish or draw executed.

- [079 Deathmatch Arena](review-evidence/deathmatch079-global-attack-permission-native-before.json): actual Landmark play plus four live responses still leaves the normal attack chooser at adjacent P2/P4, omitting permitted P3 after six inputs. Bounded FaB shared eligibility omission; no P3 answer, attack or damage executed.

All seven are independently confirmed, unfixed, uncredited and have no saved passing fixture. Gameplay repairs remain pending authorization; this continuation has kept runtime0d87 unchanged. The full [issue ledger](card-regression-issues.json) separates these from qualified branches and source-only concerns. No fusion answer, retargeted Hand1 workaround, failed suffix or untested pitch sibling is counted.


Checkpoint through batch218: **3,429 saved and registered scenarios: 3,074 duels and 355 genuine four-live cases.** Recorded primary subjects span **588/5,196 exact identities and 462/3,324 pitch cycles or standalone groups**; 353 groups have four-live profiles. Printings are metadata. Untested pitch siblings and support cards receive no primary credit; these are representative branches, not full-card completion.

**Through218 historical repair summary.** Five new distinct gameplay defect groups cover Mauvrion Skies' earned hit layer, Phantasmal Haze's destruction layer/Phantasm priority, Briar's first attack-action damage layer, preservation of the resolving source across effect-pitch callbacks, and Cintari's defended-trigger response window. Dreadbore extended the existing `managed_virtual_lifetime` family; Blood Tribute extended `named_plain_lifecycle`. See [issues043-049](card-regression-issues.json). Through218 task gameplay changes were under `FaBSim/Custom`; no task Core changes were made. Later051 corrects the canonical Gauntlet producer and regenerates its one boolean through the guarded existing compiler, with source and output checks. The user's separate Auth commit is preserved. Original Cintari recording/components/ROS compatibility retain their f3 context; its fresh current-runtime qualification is explicitly separate. Cintari's old physical buff target/duration was already correct and is not counted as another defect.

Aether Sink's source-context repair has 12 actual-function contracts /68 assertions and focused barrier checks. One legacy Wellingtons snapshot changed only its stored source-reference leaf, preserving initial/actions/assertions/meta. Cintari has 11 actual-function component groups/20 checks, correct native before-failure/after-pass evidence and an existing ROS witness; end-turn expiry and physical source Stack detours remain untested. Shared component proofs do not certify every card body or timing class.

Batches 217-218 add Creepers' earned AP0 play-as-instant permission, Quelling Robe's real static payment/prevention, Storm Striders' real off-turn/AP0 permission, and Recital's actual paid next-attack power. Four scoped controls detected the intended failures while retaining all boundary/suffix assertions. Quell's control loses static availability before funding, including conditional payment; it is not a prevention-only quantity control. Recital's control proves power sensitivity on a fully blocked attack; hit/bottom sensitivity is untested. Delayed destruction, expiry, other targets/prerequisites and untested siblings receive no credit.

**No full suite was run in this breadth phase.** The user's broad verification remains `& 'C:\xampp\php\php.exe' DevTools/RunIntegrationTests.php --root=FaBSim`. Focused evidence does not establish full merge readiness. Native helper controls now total 117 historically, 33 from this phase at their actual contexts. Positive-only cases explicitly receive no isolated fault credit; native before/after repair evidence is counted separately. Recipe/protocol/printed-stat/counter/naming corrections are not gameplay defect groups.

[Source-only holds](sparse-source-only-holds.json) remain separate from confirmed failures and qualified branches. Hides' target timing, Soul Food's self-movement and Metacarpus Node's WHEN-play timing remain unattempted/uncredited. Later source packets receive no coverage before native qualification. Work continues locally, preserving existing fixtures and unrelated user work. The assistant has not staged, committed, pushed or deployed. Compact proof and reusable components stay in the repository; fuller authoring/recovery records stay in the task workspace.

The existing Arsenal case also passes unchanged. This adds one bug scenario and zero new catalog groups. The shared MPG restriction consumers and Craterhoof remain unchanged. Remote authoring still contains the old flag; the reviewed local compile hook remains necessary. Full output archives and rollback copies stay outside the repository.

Stand Ground226 adds one standalone group: real Might+Vigor yield two defense against actual attack4, dealing two damage to P4. All four seats participate. The case stops at DAMAGE with combat open; Temper/closure and token creation are untested. Aether Conduit and Beseech the Demigon are source-only timing holds with no native failure or coverage credit. - **FAB-REG-142, Pursue:** before, HP16 was reached but its required responseable hit layer was absent. `FaBSim/Custom/WTRAbilities.php` adds a shared captured-Mark primitive and one explicit card mapping to the existing hit pipeline. It captures the hit Hero and resolves after responses; existing token payloads and factories are retained. [Qualification](card-regression-sparse568-pursue142-9fbaa-checkpoint.json).

Source review excludes auto-binding: both adjacent targets 1/3 were legal, and auto-binding would still have started play. The permission carrier is omitted by the FaB shared combat-window reset. The correct R2 probe executed 71 native inputs and passed 5,569 queries before failing query 5,570. Another 1,879 queries, future payment and damage were **unrun**. Direct proof of the banished card is pre-click; its post-click Banish-specific rows were also unrun. No fixture was saved. Earlier grant timing, later permission expiry and globally distinct root attribution remain unqualified. No gameplay repair was attempted or applied. The [issue ledger](card-regression-issues.json) appended row 155 while preserving all prior 154 rows; row count is not a distinct-bug count. Its exact no-save reproducer is:
